## Completed bounded batch

Twenty Aurma focus descriptions replaced developer shorthand, unsupported mechanic promises or unclear prose with concise maritime text matching their direct rewards; two titles corrected: Sea of Thieves and An End to an Era. IDs, prerequisites, costs, rewards, events and icons remain unchanged. All twenty reward blocks were read in full: none calls an event/decision. Sea of Blood grants political power/war support at a stability cost, not subject status. Sea of Thieves grants experience/war support, not territorial claims. Waves of Resilience gives manpower/experience, not an invasion-duration modifier. Freljordian Boarding Parties has no coastal-ownership condition and gives manpower/experience, not a special invasion capability. Pirate Bay's dockyard is conditional on an owned controlled coastal state; its text now reflects that condition. Island Ownership increases authority/stability without transferring land. End to an Era does not launch the previously advertised Pirate Royalty quest.

## Voice and source discipline

Aurma: practical, proud maritime councils and captains, trade/security balanced against raiding and domestic unrest. Its boarding-party recruitment and political routes are mod-authored scenarios, not canon claims. Official source used for the limited Freljord hardship/warrior premise: https://universe.leagueoflegends.com/en_US/region/freljord/ . It does not establish an Aurma recruitment treaty.

Provisional guides awaiting source review: Shurima imperial restoration and contested legitimacy; Xerath domination and ascension; Nazumah independent hunter culture; Targon distinct celestial/devotional factions; Icathian mortals distinct from the inhuman Void. Shakkal, shared campaigns and raiders require their own source-derived guides; do not reuse Aurma's voice.

Propose those as separate possible authored expansions only after the actual Trello intent is accessible; do not silently implement them. Current text describes the existing rewards without removing these possibilities from the design backlog.

## Second run - 7 October 2026, 06:58 Stockholm

Baseline remains main c4400497f55266833e6f49e24148eee54e74fa87. Earlier owned changes were present and preserved; the other visible local Azir chat was idle. Rewrote 31 descriptions and corrected six titles (Naval Lineage, capitalization and the two embedded line-break spacings); all keys/IDs and mechanics are preserved. Earlier twenty descriptions were preserved.

National Identity adds a spirit with no modifier, so the text announces identity without promising a statistical benefit. Naval Outposts improves construction speed and does not create outposts immediately. Warships affects dockyard output/capital-ship attack and grants experience, rather than spawning ships. Sea of Iron supports industry, not an automatic anti-piracy victory. Clan Medarda gives trade opinion/stability modifiers, not a faction invitation or alliance. Fisheries' dockyard requires an owned controlled coastal state. Ocean Stewardship's service theme remains but no tourist or guest system is promised. Open-Water Tyrants and the two Supremacy descriptions now express ambitions rather than a guaranteed territorial outcome. Both family/trade routes retain their distinct connections-versus-commercial-merit emphasis.

Official primary source consulted for Medarda's established place in Piltover: https://universe.leagueoflegends.com/en_US/region/piltover/?mv=image-gallery-1 . Wording deliberately avoids naming a contemporary clan leader or importing an Arcane chronology into the mod's 990 scenario. Aurma's government, recruitment and diplomatic paths remain authored scenarios; the source does not establish an in-game alliance. Targonian Navigators now refers to the authored navigators' experience without an unsupported specific stellar ability claim.
